HANDED TO CODEX — two jobs, 16 Oct 2026 about 11:10 AEST, on Andrew's "Talk to Codex now". Owner Codex once claimed here; brief on PR #1.

1. **Stop the sync doubling list records (priority).** Root cause found in the live page (v10.37): `mergeRecords(mine, theirs)` — called on every live snapshot fold (`const {merged, report} = mergeRecords(S, theirs); S = merged;` in the sync apply path) — unions the list collections (`union('fenceDockets','dockets')`, `union('variances',…)`, `union('serviceNotes',…)`): when the same id is held in both copies with different content it keeps BOTH, re-keyed `base·h4(JSON)`, and records a "clash". The page then writes the merged list back, so one stale tab doubles every record it disagrees on. That is exactly 14 Oct (36592) and 16 Oct 10:12 AEST (6699 → 6749: all sixteen of 36601–36616 doubled, the stale copy carrying the pre-09:23 note "Owner sign-off: Andrew Fisher", the current one "Andrew"; 36592's old reading back under the plain id; no stamps written, no `by`). The keep-both rule is right for an import of two people's files; it is wrong for the live sync, where the service is the authority and each list record has its own stamp (`stamps/fenceDockets/<id>`): the later stamp should win as it does for map and value collections, and a stale tab must never write a re-keyed twin back. Wanted: the fix as a DRAFT version with a test that reproduces the 10:12 doubling from the backups (`record_16Oct2026_hire_agreements_36601_36616/backup_state_20261016T003306Z.json` is the doubled record; `…T232819Z.json` the clean one before) and proves it no longer happens; GET-only tests; nothing on the live record; no upload without Andrew's "Upload". Also worth a look: after de-duplication the page renames a surviving `base·hash` back to `base` on load; if `base` carries a tombstone the record vanishes (36592 did at 10:34, fixed at 6899).

2. **No agent names on any tab (if there is room).** Andrew's standing rule: nothing on any tab may say an entry was made by Codex or Claude. Old records still carry "Andrew Fisher via Codex" / "via Claude" in `by`, `recorded_by` and history entries (e.g. delivery/T0158, labour/T0103, fenceDockets/F-AFV-0035 `recorded_by`), and the page shows them in drawers, history lists and prints. Fix the display (show the person only); do not rewrite the record.

(5) Broadcast kit FL03, FL04, TM01 (6932 → 6956, `record_16Oct2026_broadcast_kit/report_20261016T003953Z.json`). Lesson for the next de-duplication: never set a copy aside under the plain id while a suffixed twin survives — the page renames the survivor onto the plain id and the tombstone eats it; set the suffixed copy aside or put back and correct in place. `settle_rekey.cjs` (lets the page write that tidy alone) is kept for next time, dry-run only.

CLAIMED — v10.25 Milestones: a 100% celebration and a generated start-to-finish summary per area, 15 Oct 2026 about 09:20 AEST. Owner Claude; builds on live `681da5c7…`. Andrew, 15 Oct 2026 about 09:15 AEST in Claude's chat: "when something reaches 100% we need to do an animation almost like a celebration fireworks over it… And a summary on start to finish from 0-100%… this needs to be automated no stories just generated data based on what has happened in that area… Example now. Read first: this board, `handover_claude_15Oct2026/README.md`, PR #1 6172736470 (Claude's checklist to Codex, posted 02:08 AEST) and everything after it to 6173708414; nothing new on PR #1 since. Live by the view URL at 03:08 AEST: `06d6e6b0956c793299c99b5e48a7ee1c3fe16616f20a60ff0696d410f111848d` (15,901,228 bytes, 120 scripts), Codex's v10.21 as the entries below record; `/health` answers record version 6078, 449 files, server build v5.87. `toolchain/build.sh v10.21` with no patch reproduces the live page byte for byte, check_page PASS. Step 1 therefore does not apply and the `9db6edd2…` candidate stays withdrawn: its condition (live still `2d09fa44…`) failed when Codex published at 02:34 AEST, the live page already carries its three patches plus Codex's corrections, applying `patch_v1019/1020/1021` again on `06d6e6b0…` would apply them twice (each patch refuses a repeat), and `upload_page.py`'s base guard refuses the old build. Steps 2 and 3 stopped at the same fault as at 02:40 and 02:55: every request to `/api/*` from this environment answers 401 `{"error":"a view or edit token is needed"}` with `X-Proxy-Error: upstream auth failed: connection "GC500 upload access", host "gc500-production.up.railway.app"`. Pinned down this time, read-only: a request with NO token from Claude at all (no header, no query) gets the same 401 and the same proxy annotation; so does the public view token in the header, and `?t=<view token>` with no header. The live server (v5.87, `level()` in `server_v5.87_pictures_from_shared_number_LIVE/server.js`) reads `x-gc500-token` first and falls back to `?t=` only when that header is absent or empty, and every `/api` route goes through `level()` (there is no path-key API route; `/v/<key>` and `/health` answer 200 because they do not). So the proxy adds a non-empty `x-gc500-token` header to every request to that host, and its value is neither the edit key nor the view key: the header name in the "GC500 upload access" entry is right and its value is wrong, most likely a prefix such as "Bearer " in front of the key, or a stale or mistyped key. `upload_page.py --dry-run` on the no-patch build: "live page unchanged since the build started - safe to upload", `service: 401`, "that key is not the edit key - stopping before anything is written". `verify_after.py` → `STOP: GET /api/files answered 401`; `upload_prestarts.py --manifest manifest.json` (dry run) → the same 401 at `GET /api/files`; `po_checked.cjs` (dry run) → `STOP: GET /api/state answered 401`. Nothing uploaded, nothing written, no backup or report folder, no P/O tick, no pre-start uploaded or removed, native 6078 unchanged. The fix is still Andrew's, one setting: the environment's Edit screen, Network secrets, the "GC500 upload access" entry — delete it (the `GC500_EDIT_TOKEN` variable already there is enough on its own: the tools send it in the header and the proxy then leaves the header alone), or keep it with host exactly `gc500-production.up.railway.app`, header `x-gc500-token`, prefix empty, value the current edit key with no spaces around it; Save; new session. Proof before any write, in that session: `python3 toolchain/upload_page.py build/GC500_v10.21/GC500_Delivery_Control_hosted.html --dry-run` must end "the key is the edit key", and `python3 record_15Oct2026_prestarts_signed/verify_after.py` must list the files (it uses the view key, so it answers the moment the proxy stops rewriting the header). Order once that holds, on live `06d6e6b0…`, no page upload: (1) `GC500_EXPECTED_PAGE_SHA=06d6e6b0956c793299c99b5e48a7ee1c3fe16616f20a60ff0696d410f111848d node po_checked.cjs --apply` in `record_15Oct2026_prestarts_signed/`; (2) `python3 upload_prestarts.py --manifest manifest.json --apply` (33 uploads, 17 removals; the four 12–15 Oct fencing `confirm` entries included on Andrew's instruction, `--only <day>` if he changes that); (3) `python3 verify_after.py`; (4) readback here and on PR #1. v10.22 (Event nights run sheet) rebuilds on `06d6e6b0…`. Posted to PR #1 (Claude → Andrew / Codex, 15 Oct 2026 about 03:20 AEST).

QUANTITY RULE — Andrew, this chat14Oct2026: “If no qty and you have a description that is qty 1 ... if there is another description that will be qty 1 ... if there is a description that has qty 3 in it. Means qty is 3 ... you dont need to flag it or say its a issue.” v10.12 now applies this rule through shared quantity readers: retain entered quantities, read explicitly stated description quantities, otherwise one per separate described item. Ratings, dimensions, reference numbers and asset numbers are not description quantities. Confirmed received/installed records retain their separate actual quantities. The proposed missing-quantity Inventory warning work is superseded and will not be published. Root integrates the shared-rule patch and tests its cross-view and financial impact before release.

**ANDREW, 8 Oct 2026 ~03:40 AEST: truck flow and A+ quality. Claimed by Claude as v8.91 and v8.92, to build on the full release below.**

"This need to be all A+ class perfection. Every look. Every movement. Smooth. Fast. No lag. Navigation needs to be easy. Terminology needs to be correct."

**Truck flow rules (Andrew's words; a planning guide, "earlier the better"):**

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Staff on the day | They set the day's numbers |
| Day's loads | Can be reordered; labels renumber ("5 of 7" → "4 of 7") |
| Main Beach | One truck at a time |
| Pit lane | Up to 2 trucks from Week 4; up to 6 in Weeks 5 and 6 (holding) |
| Helen Park | The red line on Rankin Pde near Breaker St: up to 4 trucks parked at any one time, for any task, to keep trucks out of congested areas |
| Oversized | Up to 7 at any one time (4 Helen Park, 2 Pit lane, 1 at location) |
| Curfew loads | On the earliest run before restrictions (e.g. WC60 oversized toilets) |
| Shared trucks | Loads may share a truck (e.g. generators) only where weights, dimensions and the load restraint guide allow. Don't over-complicate |

**Printed checklist:** site aware of truck status · loads all ready · truck doors loaded on the correct side · trucks staggered. Whoever prints shows as "Printed by <first and last name>" on the daily run and on every attached sheet. Drivers must turn up in their allocated order.

**The two builds:**
- **v8.91 (truck flow):** daily runs, Drivers/Install prints, crew, ordering, area limits, curfew-first and shared-truck suggestions.
- **v8.92 (A+ pass):** measured speed and jank fixes, look, navigation, terminology across every other part of the page.

## STANDING INSTRUCTION — Andrew, 2 Oct 2026 16:05 AEST: "Put pressure on codex to do more and use more agents to get 10/10 out of them for building more and getting more of an elite feel. They do more into the build"

**The bar for every release from now on (both agents): 10/10, elite.** A release is not READY until all of these hold:
1. Andrew's design rules (AGENTS.md): components kept as built, every fact once, no dead space, mock-up on the real page and his yes before any new layout.
2. Measured, not eyeballed: empty card area per section (target under 15% on a 1,440 px laptop), page length before and after, tab open time (median of 6, no worse than live).
3. Both 21-tab/7-link sweeps clean, the release's own practice tests, and the standing suites (navigation, rules, fresh-after-save, packed Today, Equipment).
4. Phone and desktop pictures looked at before anyone says done.
5. An independent review by a separate agent before READY, and its findings fixed or answered in the README.

**Use parallel agents** for every release: per-tab auditors, a test writer, a separate reviewer, a phone checker. One agent doing everything in sequence is not the standard.

**Codex's build list** (on top of the v7.94 Showcase and the record entries in the alignment below), in this order; claim each on this board first:

| # | Work | What 10/10 looks like |
|---|---|---|
| C1 | **Upload v7.96 today** (or v7.94 then v7.96 rebuilt) | Andrew sees the one-tab Today and Equipment live |
| C2 | **Record entries today**: fencing 36564–36568, 24461–24465; Tue/Wed delivery notes | fencing figures current to 2 Oct; notes on the day views and driver sheets |
| C3 | **Timeline**: same treatment as Today (repeats off, no dead space, packed cards, phone first) + Print for drivers / Print for installers (one page per load, reference hero, picture, pinned location, QR) | measured before/after; driver sheet tested on paper and phone |
| C4 | **Documents** tab: one look with Equipment and Today, every repeat off, the fencing papers findable by docket number | measured; every file opens |
| C5 | **Fencing** and **Costs** tabs: the same clean-up; every $ traces to a docket, contract line or card rate; nothing shown twice across Today, Equipment, Fencing, Costs | a cross-tab repeat report with zero unexplained repeats |
| C6 | **Showcase** day/night look and the Machine view: no wall through the car, nothing blocking any angle, no lag | frame-rate and visual checks on desktop and phone |

**Claude's list** (claimed on this board as they start): Map explorer and Coates Way with the same treatment; the cross-tab repeat checker every release runs; rebuilds of v7.96 on whatever goes live; review of Codex's releases on request.

Each agent posts on PR #1 when it claims, when it is READY, and when it is LIVE. Andrew should never have to ask "when".

## Claimed — being worked on now

**Demob rules still to build (from Andrew, relayed by Codex on PR #1, comment 5966807000, 3 Oct 2026):**
- "we can only ever be at 2 locations at any one time": at most two active locations at once. This limits locations, not trucks or units.
- "please make note of what needs a crane": a crane truck needed to remove an item is recorded separately from a crane booked on site.
- "with the sewer tank if there is a toilet block ontop it requires a crane truck to take off the toilet block. Once thats gone we can then do the waste tank": a tank under a block stays blocked until the block has actually been removed. A scheduled pick-up time is not proof of removal.
- None of these is enforced by the live page yet. Codex owns the equipment and demob register. Any build waits for Andrew's yes.

  or the Brisbane branches' (KINP, NVAC, MEAD, STPS)? (2) Transport: are the delivery and pickup charges to the V8s still
  to go on the contracts, or is transport inside the event rates? The contracts carry $6,938 against $22,011 already paid
  to carriers (Transport Recovery 0.32 against the business's 1.06). (3) Wages: the crew's install hours charged to the
  job (2143) or branch staff cost (3210)? (4) The fencing card rate's split between hire and installation — for Advanced.
  Then the eight v7.68 proposals in §4 (wording and grouping only) — yes or no.

  2026, or send the card for a release.

  later" — v7.49 charges the Q6844 servicing at the card's pump-out rates. Still open: (a) the water truck, pre-fill,

  (generator rule, now in AGENTS.md) — v7.49 charges the plant from the card; the fork extension and tyne rotator
  have no card line. (c) MEAD 9968726 line 1 (2.5 t RT forklift): Rate 1 $1,483.20 is exactly

  barriers $1.18 are true daily rates; MEAD's 5 t at $405 a day looks genuine.)
